          ;(function () {

            if(/OSW_PC/i.test(window.navigator.userAgent)){
              return 
            }
            var visibilityHidden = false
            function installPerformanceObserverPatch() {
              var nativePerformanceObserver = window.PerformanceObserver
              if (!nativePerformanceObserver) return
              // 重写了PerformanceObserver
              window.PerformanceObserver = class extends nativePerformanceObserver {
                constructor(callback) {
                  super((list, observer) => {
                    var entries = list.getEntries()
                    var entry = entries[entries.length - 1]
                    // 当页面加载中 用户切走页面，LCP 可能会记录一个较大的值，导致误报，所以当 LCP 事件发生时，如果页面不可见，则不触发回调
                    if (visibilityHidden) {
                      window.isLCPMonitorPerformanceObserver = true 
                      return
                    }
                    // 8s以上的异常数据过滤掉
                    if (entry && (entry.entryType === 'largest-contentful-paint') && entry.startTime > 8000) {
                      return
                    }
                    callback && callback(list, observer)
                  })
                }
              }
            }
            function handleVisibilityChange() {
              visibilityHidden = true
              document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange)
            }
            document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange)
            installPerformanceObserverPatch()
          })()
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('自定义Performance失败:', error)
        }
    </script>

            appId: 'f06cc',

              // 签名可用率监控，业务方自行上报

                try {
                  window.dra &&
                    window.dra.sendCustomEvent &&
                    window.dra.sendCustomEvent({
                      name: 'main_search',
                      metrics: {
                        error_code: '751',

                      },

                        error_code: res.code,
                      },
                    })

                }
              }
            },
            onRequestTokenRemotely: (res) => {
              // 算法接口可用率监控，业务方自行上报
              if (res.code != 200) {
                try {
                  window.dra &&

                        error_code: '751',

                      },

                        error_msg: res && res.message ? res.message : '接口加密失败',
                      },
                    })
                } catch (error) {
                  console.log(error)
                }
              }
            },
          })
        } catch (error) {
          console.log('ParamsSign', error)

  function clickReport() {
    $("body").delegate("[poi]", "click", function (e) {
      let $current = $(e.target);
      let tagName = $current.prop("tagName");

      if (tagName === "A" || tagName === "a") {
        let fullpoi = $current.attr("poi")
          ? $current.attr("poi")
          : $current.parents("[poi]").attr("poi");
        let url = $current.attr("href");
        let text = $.trim($current.text());

        window.footerGetOnClick && window.footerGetOnClick(fullpoi, url, text);
      }
    });
  }
  clickReport();
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
  function footerRender() {
    function getClstagPrefix() {
      var $clstagEles = $("[clstag]");
      $clstagEles.each(function () {
        var fullpoi = $(this).attr("clstag");
        $(this).attr("clstag", pageConfig.clstagPrefix + fullpoi);
      });
    }

    function getCopyrightTxt() {
      var $copyrightEles = $(".copyright_txt");
      $copyrightEles.html(
        "Copyright&nbsp;©&nbsp;2004&nbsp;-&nbsp;" +
          new Date().getFullYear() +
          "&nbsp;&nbsp;京东JINGDONG&nbsp;版权所有"
      );
    }

    getClstagPrefix();
    getCopyrightTxt();
  }

  footerRender();
</script>
